on failure git clone --depth 1 the repo); log n_records per file\\nfor each record: split raw_logic_programs[0] into sections; lines matching '(.+?):::(.+)' -> (fol, nl); keep role premise|conclusion\\ncurated FOLIO rows: conclusions (id not startswith 'story') -> ref = FOL_sentence (corrected), amb flag\\nagreed premises: FOLIO-validation premise rows (the curated jsonl 'story' rows supply original premise text+FOL; if absent, use folio_refined_validation.csv's original column) matched by norm(text) to folio-refined premise text; agreed iff equivalent_modulo_vocab(orig, refined).status in {EQUIV, VOCAB}; ref = the refined formula\\nTRACK L: every (system, nl, fol) whose norm(nl) matches a curated conclusion (ref = corrected gold) or an agreed premise (ref = agreed formula)\\n   match-rate report: distinct conclusions matched / total. IF distinct matched conclusions < 150: extend ONLY with premises of matched stories whose original and refined gold agree (as the strategy states), log it\\nTRACK H: 302 curated items; candidate = original gold, ref = corrected; label via label(); ambiguity==True and label != CORRECT -> READING_CHOICE\\nHREF (NOT in screen_items.json; this experiment only): the corrected gold formula itself as candidate, label CORRECT_REF, for known-negative calibration and the L3 gate\\nitem_id = sha1(system + '|' + norm(text) + '|' + candidate_fol)[:16]   (system for track H = 'human_original')\\nstrata: words = len(text.split()) -> '<12'|'12-19'|'>=20'; n_quant = count(∀,∃) in the REFERENCE; depth = max nesting of binary connectives+quantifiers in the reference AST; n_conditions = number of atoms in antecedent/restrictor positions of the reference (conjuncts under the left of ->/↔ inside ∀); exception = regex on text (unless|except|excluding|but not|without|other than|provided that)\\n   Strata use the reference, not the candidate, so they are identical across experiments. Record this choice in screen_meta.\\nINVARIANCE SET: the first 150 track-L CORRECT items by item_id sort. Rewrite families (seed 0):\\n   RENAME: each predicate/constant -> a WordNet synonym of its head CamelCase token (first lemma of the first synset that differs from the word; else prefix 'Is'), same arity\\n   REORDER: commute every and/or operand; DEMORGAN: rewrite ¬(A∧B) <-> ¬A∨¬B at the first site, or introduce ¬¬ plus De Morgan on a conjunction if none exists\\n   PRENEX: pull quantifiers outward where legal (the variable is not free in the sibling); CONTRAPOSITIVE: A→B => ¬B→¬A at the top implication under the quantifier block\\n   Every rewrite except RENAME must be z3-verified equivalent to the original; if it is not, or the family does not apply, record n/a, never drop silently.\\nwrite screen_items.json: [{item_id, track, system, role, story_id, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, label, auto_class, repair_ops, ambiguous, strata{...}}]\\n   plus screen_meta {n per track/label/system, match_rate, labels_sha256 = sha256(json.dumps(sorted((id,label)))), code shas}\\n   plus invariance_set.json [{item_id, family, rewritten_fol, verified_equiv}]\\n\\n=== STAGE 3: FOL-Triage functions (src/fol_triage.py), each with a precise docstring ===\\nfol_lint(fol:str) -> {'codes': sorted smell codes, 'parse_ok', 'z3_unknown'}\\n   = iter-3 smells() verbatim (frozen); z3 checks at 1500 ms; exception -> the z3-dependent smells are marked UNKNOWN, never silently absent\\ncontent_accounting(text, fol) -> {'uncarried_frac','unanchored_preds','n_content','n_preds', 'flag'}  # iter-3 code; flag iff unanchored>=1 or uncarried_frac>0.34 (iter-3 thresholds)\\nformula_role_profile(fol) -> per predicate key 'Name/arity':\\n   mono = fol.profile(e, ms=3000)  (exact: UP/DOWN/NONMONO/VACUOUS/UNKNOWN)\\n   pos = 'antecedent' if some occurrence sits in the left of → or the left of a restrictor ↔ under a ∀ block, else 'consequent'\\n   local_neg = odd count of explicit ¬ between the atom and its antecedent/consequent root (syntactic)\\n   role = derived from (mono, local_neg): DOWN&¬neg -> condition; UP&¬neg -> asserted; DOWN&neg -> negated-asserted; UP&neg -> negated-condition/exception; NONMONO -> 'mixed'\\n   force = quantifier of the outermost block binding any argument variable: ∀->all, ∃->some, constant-only -> named\\n   claim = index of the top-level conjunct (prenex_blocks), split further by variable-connectivity components (union-find as in connectivity_flags)\\n   slots = argument tuple (variables mapped to their restrictor predicates, constants as names)\\nrole_accounting(text, fol) -> {'cond_in_up','assert_in_down','pol_flip','slot_swap','n_role_words','n_resolved','flag','codes'}\\n   doc = spaCy(text). CONDITION-role tokens: tokens in the subtree of an advcl whose mark ∈ {if, when, whenever, once, provided, as long as, in case}; relcl subtrees attached to a subject noun (who/that/which); a generic subject head noun + amod/compound (plural bare nsubj, or det ∈ {all, every, each, any, no}; 'everyone/anyone who' -> relcl). EXCEPTION tokens: under 'unless/except/without/but not/other than'. ASSERTION tokens: root verb/acomp/attr/dobj outside the condition subtrees.\\n   Only when the sentence is generic/universal (condition tokens exist) apply the role rules; existential ('some', 'there is') and named-individual sentences get only the polarity + slot rules.\\n   align each role token to predicates with content_accounting.lex (Porter stems + WordNet lemmas/synonyms/derivations), SORTAL/HEDGE exemptions\\n   expected mono: condition -> DOWN, assertion -> UP, exception -> UP (∀x(A∧¬E→B)); flip once if the token is negated (neg dep, 'no', 'never', 'not', 'none', n't; 'no' as a subject det flips the assertion only)\\n   cond_in_up = condition tokens whose aligned predicates are ALL strictly opposite (UP) ; assert_in_down similarly ; pol_flip = negated/non-negated mismatch counted separately; NONMONO/UNKNOWN never fire\\n   slot_swap: for a verb with nsubj(+pass) and dobj/pobj both aligned to the args of one binary predicate aligned to the verb: active voice expects nsubj -> arg0; passive expects the reverse; a mismatch counts as a swap\\n   flag iff cond_in_up+assert_in_down+pol_flip+slot_swap >= 1\\nrole_questionnaire(text, model) -> schema JSON (TEXT ONLY; the formula is never in the prompt)\\n   SCHEMA (frozen in prereg): {'concepts':[{'id':'c1','phrase':<exact words from sentence>, 'role':'condition'|'asserted'|'background', 'negated':bool, 'exception':bool, 'force':'all'|'some'|'named'|'none', 'claim':int}], 'relations':[{'phrase':<verb/relational words>, 'first':cid, 'second':cid}]}\\n   prompt: fixed system rubric defining each field with 4 short worked examples, NOT drawn from FOLIO/MALLS (hand-written, e.g. 'Every dog that barks is loud unless it is asleep'); response_format json_object; temperature 0; max_tokens 700\\n   validate with a pydantic/jsonschema check; up to 2 retries, then coverage_status='L3_FAIL'\\nl3_compare(questionnaire, profile, text) -> per-field mismatch features:\\n   align concept phrases -> predicates (lex stems+WordNet, token-Jaccard over CamelCase tokens >= 0.5; best match; unmatched -> missing)\\n   mm_role = fraction of aligned concepts where the expected mono from (role, negated, exception) != z3 mono (strict UP/DOWN only)\\n   mm_force = fraction of aligned concepts whose force != profile force ; mm_claims = 1 - Rand index between the text claim partition and the formula claim partition over aligned predicates\\n   mm_order = fraction of relations with both args aligned whose first/second order is reversed vs the predicate slots ; mm_exception = exception concept aligned to a predicate that is not UP-under-∀ or negated in the antecedent\\n   n_missing = concepts with role in {condition, asserted} and no predicate ; n_extra = predicates matched by no concept (SORTAL exempt)\\n   l3_score = sum of GATED field mismatches (weights frozen = 1 each; normalised by n concepts)\\nfol_triage(text, fol) -> {'p_error', 'fired_layer' ∈ {L1,L2,L3,none}, 'codes', 'error_code', 'coverage_status'}\\n   cascade order L1 -> L2(bow, role) -> L3; fired_layer = the first layer whose own frozen threshold is exceeded; p_error = fused logistic\\n   error_code map (frozen): EX_IMP->RESTR, ALL_AND->RESTR, IFF_RESTR->CONN, GLUE->UNGLUE, FREE->BIND, ARITY->BIND, DANGLING->BIND, VACUOUS->DROP, TRIVIAL->COMPOUND, unanchored->ADD, uncarried->DROP, cond_in_up->RESTR, pol_flip->NEG, slot_swap->SWAP, mm_force->QUANT, mm_claims->UNGLUE, mm_role->REV, mm_order->SWAP, n_missing->DROP, n_extra->ADD\\n\\n=== STAGE 4: PRE-REGISTRATION (before ANY track-L label is joined) ===\\nprereg.json = {timestamp_utc, sha256 of src/*.py, smell list, L2 thresholds, role-rule lexicons, L3 schema + prompt text + model id, field gate (>=0.85 target, <0.70 excluded), fusion feature list, fusion C=1.0, operating thresholds, analyses list, bootstrap B=2000 clustered by story_id/sentence, strata, prevalences {0.10,0.25}}\\nSCORING CODE loads screen items through a view WITHOUT label/auto_class/repair_ops fields (assert those keys are absent); only analysis.py joins labels.\\n\\n=== STAGE 5: calibration on known negatives (track H + HREF only) ===\\nrun L3 on all unique track-H sentences (<=302 calls); z3 profile on HREF references\\nL3 GATE: per-field accuracy = agreement of questionnaire answers with formula_role_profile(corrected gold) over aligned concepts on HREF items (report n aligned, alignment rate, and per-field accuracy with Wilson CI; also per length stratum)\\n   field <0.70 -> excluded from l3_score and fusion (logged); 0.70-0.85 -> kept with a flag\\n   ALSO run a 2nd cheap model (e.g. qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct or meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct; choose the cheapest with json mode) on the same HREF items -> robustness row; the primary model is fixed in prereg before this\\n   test-retest: rerun the primary L3 on 60 HREF items -> field-level agreement\\nOPERATING THRESHOLDS fixed here: L1 any smell; L2 flags as defined; L3 threshold = its 90th percentile on HREF (10% FA by construction on known negatives); fused threshold = the value giving 10% FA on HREF+track-H CORRECT (out-of-fold)\\nFUSION: features = [l1_any, n_smells, l2_bow_uncarried, l2_bow_unanch, l2_role_count, gated l3 fields..., log(n_words)]\\n   sklearn LogisticRegression(penalty='l2', C=1.0, class_weight='balanced'); positives = track-H ERROR (depth 1-2) + COMPOUND (non-ambiguous); negatives = track-H CORRECT + HREF; GroupKFold(5) by sentence -> OOF p on H; refit on all H -> frozen model saved (results/fusion_H.pkl + coefficients in JSON)\\n   write the calibration numbers into prereg.json (appended, timestamped) BEFORE stage 6\\n\\n=== STAGE 6: score track L + invariance + ablation + contamination (labels still hidden) ===\\nz3-heavy work in ProcessPoolExecutor (spawn); LLM calls with asyncio + aiohttp, Semaphore(16), exponential backoff, cost from the response 'usage' (or tokens × listed price), ledger cache/cost_ledger.jsonl, RAISE StopBudget if cumulative > $8.0\\nfor each track-L item: l1, l2_bow, l2_role, profile, L3 (cached by sentence; the text-only answer is shared across systems for the same sentence), l3 features, p_fused (frozen H model), fired_layer, error_code, seconds per layer\\ninvariance: score all layers on each rewritten formula (L3 text answers reused -> zero LLM cost); FA at the operating threshold per family and flip rate vs the original\\nDECOMPOSED JUDGE ablation: the same primary LLM sees sentence + formula, prompt: 'for each predicate in the formula answer role/negated/force/claim/arg-order AS THE FORMULA STATES IT' -> LLM formula profile; compare to (i) the z3 profile (formula-reading accuracy per field: the LLM's error on something z3 answers exactly) and (ii) the text questionnaire -> mismatch features -> fused variant 'cascade_llmformula' (refit on H with the same protocol). Run it on track H, HREF and track L.\\nCONTAMINATION: nonce-disguise every track-L and HREF sentence: content lemmas (NOUN/PROPN/VERB/ADJ, not in FUNC/logic words) -> pronounceable CVCVC nonces from sha1(lemma) (consistent within an item); apply the same lemma->nonce map to the CamelCase tokens of the reference/candidate predicate names -> disguised formula. Run L3 on the disguised text; report per-field accuracy vs the z3 profile of the disguised reference (HREF + track-L agreed refs), original vs disguised, and track-L AUROC of l3_score original vs disguised (paired bootstrap). Also report the alignment rate (WordNet cannot help nonces; use exact-token alignment in both conditions for the comparison).\\n\\n=== STAGE 7: analysis (src/analysis.py; labels joined here) ===\\nprimary set: track L, label ∈ {CORRECT, ERROR}; tie-aware pairwise accuracy acc_eq with tie calibration (Deutsch et al. 2023): over all item pairs, agreement of sign(y_i-y_j) with sign_ε(s_i-s_j); ε picked on track H, reported on L\\nL1 per Logic-LM system × length stratum: FA on CORRECT (Wilson CI), per-smell FA; smell FAILS if FA > 3% on any system's CORRECT items (reported, not removed); L1 precision on track-L errors\\nper-type sensitivity: for track-L ERROR items and track-H census classes, sensitivity of each layer at the operating threshold by repair operator (NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, MOVE, DROP, ADD, SWAP, BIND, SCOPE, UNGLUE, COMPOUND)\\nerror-type readout: for depth-1 ERROR items, P(error_code ∈ true ops) per layer\\ncoverage: fraction of items with every layer scored; Generate mini/preview with aii-json and check sizes with aii-file-size-limit.\\nREADME.md + .aii/manifest.yaml (keep results/, cache/ [LLM answers cost money], screen_items.json; delete .venv/ and spaCy model dirs as redownloadable)\",", "+  \"implementation_pseudocode\": \"LAYOUT (all under the workspace): src/fol.py (patched), src/labeller.py, src/screen.py, src/fol_triage.py (the 4 released functions + fol_triage()), src/llm.py (async OpenRouter + cost ledger), src/analysis.py, prereg.json, data/, cache/ (z3 + LLM JSON caches, jsonl keyed by sha1), results/, method_out.json, README.md, .aii/manifest.yaml. Read the aii-python, aii-parallel-computing, aii-long-running-tasks, aii-openrouter-llms and aii-json skills first. uv venv; deps: z3-solver, nltk (wordnet, omw-1.4), spacy + en_core_web_sm (fallback: stanza en), numpy, scipy, scikit-learn, pandas, aiohttp, loguru.\\n\\n=== STAGE 0: parser fix + unit tests (src/fol.py) ===\\ncopy fol.py; set PREC = {'iff':1,'imp':2,'xor':3,'or':3,'and':4}  # xor binds like or, tighter than ->\\nassert parse('A ⊕ B → C') == ('imp', ('xor',A,B), C)\\nassert parse('∀x (P(x) → Q(x)) ∧ ∀y (R(y))')[0] == 'and'   # quantifier binds next group (FOLIO convention)\\nassert equivalent(parse('¬(A ∧ B)'), parse('¬A ∨ ¬B')) is True\\nrecord fol_py_sha256 and a PRECEDENCE_FIX note in prereg.json\\n\\n=== STAGE 1: labeller (src/labeller.py), identical logic to repair_census.work ===\\ndef equivalent_modulo_vocab(cand_str, ref_str) -> {'status': EQUIV|VOCAB|GRAN|NONEQ|UNPARSEABLE|TIMEOUT, 'pmap', 'cmap'}:\\n    parse both (fail -> UNPARSEABLE); equivalent(c,r) True -> EQUIV; None -> TIMEOUT\\n    ac,pmap,cmap = align(c, r); equivalent(ac,r) -> VOCAB; gb=gran_bridge(ac,r); gb and equivalent(*gb) -> GRAN; else NONEQ\\ndef minimal_typed_repair(cand_str, ref_str, budget_s=25) -> {'ops': [..] | None, 'depth': 1|2|None, 'secs'}  # search() on aligned/bridged base\\ndef label(cand, ref) -> (label, auto_class, ops):\\n    EQUIV/VOCAB -> CORRECT ; GRAN -> UNCERTAIN (auto_class GRAN, 'vocab-borderline')\\n    NONEQ + repair depth 1-2 -> ERROR(ops) ; NONEQ + no repair -> UNCERTAIN (auto_class COMPOUND)\\n    UNPARSEABLE -> UNPARSEABLE (in every denominator) ; TIMEOUT -> UNCERTAIN (auto_class TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN)\\n  Emit auto_class next to label, so iteration 2 can remap if another experiment coded GRAN differently.\\n  Run with ProcessPoolExecutor(spawn, workers = cgroup CPUs, i.e. 4), per-item hard timeout 60 s, results cached in cache/labels.jsonl.\\n\\n=== STAGE 2: shared screen (src/screen.py) — MUST follow the strategy text exactly ===\\nnorm(t) = collapse whitespace(lower(t)) with punctuation stripped, except inner apostrophes removed as well\\nfetch the 3 Logic-LM files (requests; on failure git clone --depth 1 the repo); log n_records per file\\nfor each record: split raw_logic_programs[0] into sections; lines matching '(.+?):::(.+)' -> (fol, nl); keep role premise|conclusion\\ncurated FOLIO rows: conclusions (id not startswith 'story') -> ref = FOL_sentence (corrected), amb flag\\nagreed premises: FOLIO-validation premise rows (the curated jsonl 'story' rows supply original premise text+FOL; if absent, use folio_refined_validation.csv's original column) matched by norm(text) to folio-refined premise text; agreed iff equivalent_modulo_vocab(orig, refined).status in {EQUIV, VOCAB}; ref = the refined formula\\nTRACK L: every (system, nl, fol) whose norm(nl) matches a curated conclusion (ref = corrected gold) or an agreed premise (ref = agreed formula)\\n   match-rate report: distinct conclusions matched / total. IF distinct matched conclusions < 150: extend ONLY with premises of matched stories whose original and refined gold agree (as the strategy states), log it\\nTRACK H: 302 curated items; candidate = original gold, ref = corrected; label via label(); ambiguity==True and label != CORRECT -> READING_CHOICE\\nHREF (NOT in screen_items.json; this experiment only): the corrected gold formula itself as candidate, label CORRECT_REF, for known-negative calibration and the L3 gate\\nitem_id = sha1(system + '|' + norm(text) + '|' + candidate_fol)[:16]   (system for track H = 'human_original')\\nstrata: words = len(text.split()) -> '<12'|'12-19'|'>=20'; n_quant = count(∀,∃) in the REFERENCE; depth = max nesting of binary connectives+quantifiers in the reference AST; n_conditions = number of atoms in antecedent/restrictor positions of the reference (conjuncts under the left of ->/↔ inside ∀); exception = regex on text (unless|except|excluding|but not|without|other than|provided that)\\n   Strata use the reference, not the candidate, so they are identical across experiments. Record this choice in screen_meta.\\nINVARIANCE SET: the first 150 track-L CORRECT items by item_id sort. Rewrite families (seed 0):\\n   RENAME: each predicate/constant -> a WordNet synonym of its head CamelCase token (first lemma of the first synset that differs from the word; else prefix 'Is'), same arity\\n   REORDER: commute every and/or operand; DEMORGAN: rewrite ¬(A∧B) <-> ¬A∨¬B at the first site, or introduce ¬¬ plus De Morgan on a conjunction if none exists\\n   PRENEX: pull quantifiers outward where legal (the variable is not free in the sibling); CONTRAPOSITIVE: A→B => ¬B→¬A at the top implication under the quantifier block\\n   Every rewrite except RENAME must be z3-verified equivalent to the original; if it is not, or the family does not apply, record n/a, never drop silently.\\nwrite screen_items.json: [{item_id, track, system, role, story_id, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, label, auto_class, repair_ops, ambiguous, strata{...}}]\\n   plus screen_meta {n per track/label/system, match_rate, labels_sha256 = sha256(json.dumps(sorted((id,label)))), code shas}\\n   plus invariance_set.json [{item_id, family, rewritten_fol, verified_equiv}]\\n\\n=== STAGE 3: FOL-Triage functions (src/fol_triage.py), each with a precise docstring ===\\nfol_lint(fol:str) -> {'codes': sorted smell codes, 'parse_ok', 'z3_unknown'}\\n   = iter-3 smells() verbatim (frozen); z3 checks at 1500 ms; exception -> the z3-dependent smells are marked UNKNOWN, never silently absent\\ncontent_accounting(text, fol) -> {'uncarried_frac','unanchored_preds','n_content','n_preds', 'flag'}  # iter-3 code; flag iff unanchored>=1 or uncarried_frac>0.34 (iter-3 thresholds)\\nformula_role_profile(fol) -> per predicate key 'Name/arity':\\n   mono = fol.profile(e, ms=3000)  (exact: UP/DOWN/NONMONO/VACUOUS/UNKNOWN)\\n   pos = 'antecedent' if some occurrence sits in the left of → or the left of a restrictor ↔ under a ∀ block, else 'consequent'\\n   local_neg = odd count of explicit ¬ between the atom and its antecedent/consequent root (syntactic)\\n   role = derived from (mono, local_neg): DOWN&¬neg -> condition; UP&¬neg -> asserted; DOWN&neg -> negated-asserted; UP&neg -> negated-condition/exception; NONMONO -> 'mixed'\\n   force = quantifier of the outermost block binding any argument variable: ∀->all, ∃->some, constant-only -> named\\n   claim = index of the top-level conjunct (prenex_blocks), split further by variable-connectivity components (union-find as in connectivity_flags)\\n   slots = argument tuple (variables mapped to their restrictor predicates, constants as names)\\nrole_accounting(text, fol) -> {'cond_in_up','assert_in_down','pol_flip','slot_swap','n_role_words','n_resolved','flag','codes'}\\n   doc = spaCy(text). CONDITION-role tokens: tokens in the subtree of an advcl whose mark ∈ {if, when, whenever, once, provided, as long as, in case}; relcl subtrees attached to a subject noun (who/that/which); a generic subject head noun + amod/compound (plural bare nsubj, or det ∈ {all, every, each, any, no}; 'everyone/anyone who' -> relcl). EXCEPTION tokens: under 'unless/except/without/but not/other than'. ASSERTION tokens: root verb/acomp/attr/dobj outside the condition subtrees.\\n   Only when the sentence is generic/universal (condition tokens exist) apply the role rules; existential ('some', 'there is') and named-individual sentences get only the polarity + slot rules.\\n   align each role token to predicates with content_accounting.lex (Porter stems + WordNet lemmas/synonyms/derivations), SORTAL/HEDGE exemptions\\n   expected mono: condition -> DOWN, assertion -> UP, exception -> UP (∀x(A∧¬E→B)); flip once if the token is negated (neg dep, 'no', 'never', 'not', 'none', n't; 'no' as a subject det flips the assertion only)\\n   cond_in_up = condition tokens whose aligned predicates are ALL strictly opposite (UP) ; assert_in_down similarly ; pol_flip = negated/non-negated mismatch counted separately; NONMONO/UNKNOWN never fire\\n   slot_swap: for a verb with nsubj(+pass) and dobj/pobj both aligned to the args of one binary predicate aligned to the verb: active voice expects nsubj -> arg0; passive expects the reverse; a mismatch counts as a swap\\n   flag iff cond_in_up+assert_in_down+pol_flip+slot_swap >= 1\\nrole_questionnaire(text, model) -> schema JSON (TEXT ONLY; the formula is never in the prompt)\\n   SCHEMA (frozen in prereg): {'concepts':[{'id':'c1','phrase':<exact words from sentence>, 'role':'condition'|'asserted'|'background', 'negated':bool, 'exception':bool, 'force':'all'|'some'|'named'|'none', 'claim':int}], 'relations':[{'phrase':<verb/relational words>, 'first':cid, 'second':cid}]}\\n   prompt: fixed system rubric defining each field with 4 short worked examples, NOT drawn from FOLIO/MALLS (hand-written, e.g. 'Every dog that barks is loud unless it is asleep'); response_format json_object; temperature 0; max_tokens 700\\n   validate with a pydantic/jsonschema check; up to 2 retries, then coverage_status='L3_FAIL'\\nl3_compare(questionnaire, profile, text) -> per-field mismatch features:\\n   align concept phrases -> predicates (lex stems+WordNet, token-Jaccard over CamelCase tokens >= 0.5; best match; unmatched -> missing)\\n   mm_role = fraction of aligned concepts where the expected mono from (role, negated, exception) != z3 mono (strict UP/DOWN only)\\n   mm_force = fraction of aligned concepts whose force != profile force ; mm_claims = 1 - Rand index between the text claim partition and the formula claim partition over aligned predicates\\n   mm_order = fraction of relations with both args aligned whose first/second order is reversed vs the predicate slots ; mm_exception = exception concept aligned to a predicate that is not UP-under-∀ or negated in the antecedent\\n   n_missing = concepts with role in {condition, asserted} and no predicate ; n_extra = predicates matched by no concept (SORTAL exempt)\\n   l3_score = sum of GATED field mismatches (weights frozen = 1 each; normalised by n concepts)\\nfol_triage(text, fol) -> {'p_error', 'fired_layer' ∈ {L1,L2,L3,none}, 'codes', 'error_code', 'coverage_status'}\\n   cascade order L1 -> L2(bow, role) -> L3; fired_layer = the first layer whose own frozen threshold is exceeded; p_error = fused logistic\\n   error_code map (frozen): EX_IMP->RESTR, ALL_AND->RESTR, IFF_RESTR->CONN, GLUE->UNGLUE, FREE->BIND, ARITY->BIND, DANGLING->BIND, VACUOUS->DROP, TRIVIAL->COMPOUND, unanchored->ADD, uncarried->DROP, cond_in_up->RESTR, pol_flip->NEG, slot_swap->SWAP, mm_force->QUANT, mm_claims->UNGLUE, mm_role->REV, mm_order->SWAP, n_missing->DROP, n_extra->ADD\\n\\n=== STAGE 4: PRE-REGISTRATION (before ANY track-L label is joined) ===\\nprereg.json = {timestamp_utc, sha256 of src/*.py, smell list, L2 thresholds, role-rule lexicons, L3 schema + prompt text + model id, field gate (>=0.85 target, <0.70 excluded), fusion feature list, fusion C=1.0, operating thresholds, analyses list, bootstrap B=2000 clustered by story_id/sentence, strata, prevalences {0.10,0.25}}\\nSCORING CODE loads screen items through a view WITHOUT label/auto_class/repair_ops fields (assert those keys are absent); only analysis.py joins labels.\\n\\n=== STAGE 5: calibration on known negatives (track H + HREF only) ===\\nrun L3 on all unique track-H sentences (<=302 calls); z3 profile on HREF references\\nL3 GATE: per-field accuracy = agreement of questionnaire answers with formula_role_profile(corrected gold) over aligned concepts on HREF items (report n aligned, alignment rate, and per-field accuracy with Wilson CI; also per length stratum)\\n   field <0.70 -> excluded from l3_score and fusion (logged); 0.70-0.85 -> kept with a flag\\n   ALSO run a 2nd cheap model (e.g. qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct or meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct; choose the cheapest with json mode) on the same HREF items -> robustness row; the primary model is fixed in prereg before this\\n   test-retest: rerun the primary L3 on 60 HREF items -> field-level agreement\\nOPERATING THRESHOLDS fixed here: L1 any smell; L2 flags as defined; L3 threshold = its 90th percentile on HREF (10% FA by construction on known negatives); fused threshold = the value giving 10% FA on HREF+track-H CORRECT (out-of-fold)\\nFUSION: features = [l1_any, n_smells, l2_bow_uncarried, l2_bow_unanch, l2_role_count, gated l3 fields..., log(n_words)]\\n   sklearn LogisticRegression(penalty='l2', C=1.0, class_weight='balanced'); positives = track-H ERROR (depth 1-2) + COMPOUND (non-ambiguous); negatives = track-H CORRECT + HREF; GroupKFold(5) by sentence -> OOF p on H. PRIMARY frozen model for track L = refit on track-H + HREF rows whose norm(text) does NOT occur in track L (sentence-leakage guard: FOLIO conclusions appear in both tracks). SECONDARY = refit on all H. Both are saved (results/fusion_H_noleak.pkl, fusion_H_all.pkl + coefficients in JSON); keep <=8 features because the no-leak positives may be only ~30-40\\n   write the calibration numbers into prereg.json (appended, timestamped) BEFORE stage 6\\n\\n=== STAGE 6: score track L + invariance + ablation + contamination (labels still hidden) ===\\nz3-heavy work in ProcessPoolExecutor (spawn); LLM calls with asyncio + aiohttp, Semaphore(16), exponential backoff, cost from the response 'usage' (or tokens × listed price), ledger cache/cost_ledger.jsonl, RAISE StopBudget if cumulative > $8.0\\nfor each track-L item: l1, l2_bow, l2_role, profile, L3 (cached by sentence; the text-only answer is shared across systems for the same sentence), l3 features, p_fused (frozen H model), fired_layer, error_code, seconds per layer\\ninvariance: score all layers on each rewritten formula (L3 text answers reused -> zero LLM cost); FA at the operating threshold per family and flip rate vs the original\\nDECOMPOSED JUDGE ablation: the same primary LLM sees sentence + formula, prompt: 'for each predicate in the formula answer role/negated/force/claim/arg-order AS THE FORMULA STATES IT' -> LLM formula profile; compare to (i) the z3 profile (formula-reading accuracy per field: the LLM's error on something z3 answers exactly) and (ii) the text questionnaire -> mismatch features -> fused variant 'cascade_llmformula' (refit on H with the same protocol). Run it on track H, HREF and track L.\\nCONTAMINATION: nonce-disguise every track-L and HREF sentence: content lemmas (NOUN/PROPN/VERB/ADJ, not in FUNC/logic words) -> pronounceable CVCVC nonces from sha1(lemma) (consistent within an item); apply the same lemma->nonce map to the CamelCase tokens of the reference/candidate predicate names -> disguised formula. Run L3 on the disguised text; report per-field accuracy vs the z3 profile of the disguised reference (HREF + track-L agreed refs), original vs disguised, and track-L AUROC of l3_score original vs disguised (paired bootstrap). Also report the alignment rate (WordNet cannot help nonces; use exact-token alignment in both conditions for the comparison).\\n\\n=== STAGE 7: analysis (src/analysis.py; labels joined here) ===\\nprimary set: track L, label ∈ {CORRECT, ERROR}; secondary: ERROR ∪ UNCERTAIN(COMPOUND) vs CORRECT; track H reported separately; READING_CHOICE never pooled\\nper score s ∈ {l1_any, n_smells, l2_bow, l2_role, l3_score, p_fused_H, p_fused_Lcf (within-L GroupKFold by sentence, secondary), cascade_llmformula}:\\n   AUROC, AUPRC (+ prevalence), 95% CI by cluster bootstrap (resample sentences, B=2000, seed 0)\\n   paired ΔAUROC (same resamples): fused vs each layer, fused vs l2_bow (the old baseline), z3-cascade vs llmformula-cascade, fused_H vs fused_Lcf\\n   binary layers: TPR, FPR, precision, precision re-weighted: prec(π)=TPR·π/(TPR·π+FPR·(1-π)) for π=0.10, 0.25; tie-aware pairwise accuracy acc_eq with tie calibration (Deutsch et al. 2023): over all item pairs, agreement of sign(y_i-y_j) with sign_ε(s_i-s_j); ε picked on track H, reported on L\\nL1 per Logic-LM system × length stratum: FA on CORRECT (Wilson CI), per-smell FA; smell FAILS if FA > 3% on any system's CORRECT items (reported, not removed); L1 precision on track-L errors\\nper-type sensitivity: for track-L ERROR items and track-H census classes, sensitivity of each layer at the operating threshold by repair operator (NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, MOVE, DROP, ADD, SWAP, BIND, SCOPE, UNGLUE, COMPOUND)\\nerror-type readout: for depth-1 ERROR items, P(error_code ∈ true ops) per layer\\ncoverage: fraction of items with every layer scored; parse failures, z3 UNKNOWN and L3_FAIL listed separately; failed = flagged for the coverage gate\\ncomplexity: AUROC and FA per stratum (words, n_quant, depth, n_conditions, exception) with n; strata with <50 errors or <50 correct are marked UNTESTABLE (descriptive only)\\ncost: $ per item per layer (L1/L2 = $0; L3 tokens), seconds per item (p50/p95), total spend\\nGATES from the strategy's selection rule, evaluated for FOL-Triage (fused): coverage >= 90%, rewrite FA <= 10% per family, cost <= $0.002/item, AUROC >= 0.65. Report pass/fail. The ΔAUROC over the disguised judge is computed in iteration 2 by joining with experiment D on item_id.\\nwrite method_out.json per aii-json exp_gen_sol_out (validate): datasets 'screen_track_L', 'screen_track_H', 'href_calibration', each example {input: text+candidate_fol, output: label, predict_fol_triage: p_fused, metadata_*: item_id, system, strata, l1_codes, l2_bow, l2_role, l3_mismatch_fields, fired_layer, error_code, cost_usd, seconds, coverage_status, auto_class, repair_ops}. The full per-item rows are in results/per_item.jsonl; all aggregate tables are in results/metrics.json (also embedded as top-level 'metrics' if the schema permits). Generate mini/preview with aii-json and check sizes with aii-file-size-limit.\\nREADME.md + .aii/manifest.yaml (keep results/, cache/ [LLM answers cost money], screen_items.json; delete .venv/ and spaCy model dirs as redownloadable)\",", "   \"fallback_plan\": \"F1 Logic-LM fetch fails, or a file has far fewer records than FOLIO-dev: fetch via the GitHub API or a shallow git clone. If fewer than 150 distinct conclusions match after normalisation, apply the strategy's ONLY permitted extension (agreed premises of matched stories) and report the match rate. If a text fails exact normalised match because of v1/v2 wording differences, do NOT fuzzy-match: that would break identity with the other experiments. List the unmatched texts instead. F2 Premise agreement is impossible because folio_refined_validation.csv lacks original premises: build track L from conclusions only and log it. Report the matched-premise count as 0 rather than inventing a source. F3 Labeller timeouts: a per-item 60 s hard cap; the item becomes UNCERTAIN(TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN), is counted, and is never merged with proofs. If more than 10% of items time out, rerun those items once with budget_s=60 on 4 workers, then freeze. F4 spaCy role extraction is poor, i.e. under 60% of role tokens resolve to predicates on HREF: switch to stanza UD (depparse) for role_accounting only, before prereg is frozen. If still poor, report role_accounting as low-coverage and keep it out of fusion (logged). Do not tune it on track L. F5 The L3 gate fails, with every field below 0.70 on HREF: try the second cheap model and a 'one concept per predicate phrase' prompt variant, on HREF only, before freezing. If still failing, L3 is excluded from fusion, the cascade becomes L1+L2, and this is reported as a negative for text-only questionnaires with small models. If the primary model returns invalid JSON more than 10% of the time, switch to the second model and record the switch in prereg. F6 Budget: estimate first (about 3,000 calls × ~700 input + 350 output tokens ≈ $0.6-1.0 on Flash-Lite pricing $0.10/$0.40 per M, plus the second-model and decomposed runs, so about $2.5 total). The ledger raises StopBudget at $8; if hit, finish the analysis with the calls already cached and report partial coverage in the denominator. F7 Track-H fusion is badly transferred, with fused AUROC on L below the best single layer: report it as is (pre-registered primary), and report the within-L cross-fitted variant as secondary. Never swap primary and secondary after seeing the labels. F8 Too few track-L errors, fewer than 30 ERROR items: report AUROC CIs honestly (they will be wide) and add the secondary ERROR∪COMPOUND analysis. The screen is for ranking, not for claims. F9 Time pressure (6 h): the priority order is STAGE 0-2 (screen + labels, required by all), L1, L2-bow, formula_role_profile, L3, fusion, analysis; then invariance; then the decomposed judge; then contamination; then role_accounting robustness. Anything skipped is listed in method_out.json under 'not_run' with the reason.\",", "   \"testing_plan\": \"T0 (5 min): the parser unit tests from stage 0, including xor/-> bracketing. Re-run the iter-3 census on the 99 track-H pairs with the fixed parser, and confirm the counts are close to iter-3 (21 VOCAB/GRAN, 78 errors, of which about 36 are COMPOUND), with the ≈3 xor-bracketing COMPOUNDs now resolved. Diff against repair_census.rows.json and log every changed class. T1 (10 min): re-run fol_lint on the 294 parseable corrected golds and reproduce iter-3's 5/294 false alarms (after the precedence fix, small differences are logged). Reproduce content_accounting's flat FA of about 5-7% for unanchored predicates. This proves the frozen code is the same code. T2 (mini, 20 items): the screen builder on 1 Logic-LM file and 5 stories. Check parsing of the 'FOL ::: NL' lines, the item_id formula, determinism (two runs give byte-identical screen_items.json), and that labels_sha256 is stable. Hand-inspect 5 CORRECT and 5 ERROR labels (candidate vs reference printed side by side). T3 formula_role_profile sanity on hand-made formulas: ∀x(Dog(x)∧Bark(x)→Loud(x)) gives Dog, Bark DOWN/condition and Loud UP/asserted with force all and one claim. ∀x∀y(Wolf(x)∧Bird(y)→Howl(x)∧Chirp(y)) gives 2 claims. ∀x(A(x)∧¬E(x)→B(x)) gives E UP (exception). Loves(john, mary) gives named force and slots (john, mary). T4 role_accounting on 10 hand-written sentence/formula pairs, each with one planted RESTR, NEG and SWAP error and its correct version. Every error fires and no correct version fires. T5 L3 on 10 HREF sentences: valid JSON, cost per call logged, and the extrapolated total cost printed BEFORE the full sweep (abort if the projection exceeds $6). T6 Pipeline dry run on 50 items end to end (all layers, fusion with dummy folds), checking that scoring code cannot see labels (assert) and that method_out.json validates with aii-json. T7 Scale up per aii-long-running-tasks: 50 → 200 → all. Log time per item and extrapolate against the 6 h budget; z3 per-item timeouts are always on. T8 Confirmation signals before trusting results: L1 FA on HREF stays at 1.7% ± 1.5%; per-field L3 accuracy is reported; AUROC of any single layer on track H is roughly consistent with iter-3 (L2-bow paired about 0.59); the invariance RENAME family does not flip L1 (L1 should be exactly invariant to renaming, which is a built-in test: any L1 flip under renaming or reordering is a bug to fix before the run is frozen).\",", "   \"domain_practice\": \"What the field does for this exact study type (meta-evaluating a reference-free faithfulness metric on real system outputs). The sources are the ones I read or re-used this session: the strategy's domain reasoning (which condenses the neurosymbolic handbook and 2606.02837), the iter-3 probes and review (census_audit), the Logic-LM repository layout (verified), and the standard factuality and MT meta-evaluation papers named below. BASELINES. Every reference-free factuality paper compares against (i) trivial or structural scores (here: parse rate, and the user's pilot structural metrics), (ii) an entailment or round-trip score, (iii) an LLM judge, cheap and strong, and (iv) sampling self-consistency. The one a reviewer names first is the LLM judge, tuned fairly: the same rubric for every item, logprob or 0-100 scoring, and a disguised variant to control memorised gold. These live in sibling experiment D (and C for self-consistency); this experiment must emit item_id-keyed scores so the paired comparison can be done in iteration 2. Inside this experiment the fair internal baselines are the iter-3 bag-of-words accounting and an LLM reading the formula (the decomposed judge), which isolates the solver's contribution. DATA. The standard NL→FOL gold is FOLIO (validation, since test is hidden) and MALLS. Both are known to be ~39%/36% wrong as shipped (arXiv 2606.02837), so the field's current norm is to use the curated corrected gold (HF DSAVlab-UNIUD) and state it. Logic-LM (Pan et al. EMNLP-Findings 2023) released FOLIO-dev programs from three OpenAI models; these are the de-facto public 'real system outputs' for FOLIO. They are old (2023) models and FOLIO v1 wording, which is unrepresentative of 2026 systems; the 8-family held-out set (dataset E) is where the modern-family claim is tested. ProverQA is synthetic and short; SARA is Prolog, not FOL. CONTROLS HELD CONSTANT. The same items, the same labeller and the same parser for every metric; labels hidden from scoring; thresholds fixed on known negatives (clinical-screening convention: the false-positive rate is set on verified negatives, then sensitivity is read on the target population); the same prompt and model for all L3 items; temperature 0. The confound this design is most likely to be caught on is label artefacts: aligner over- and under-acceptance and correct-but-non-equivalent outputs. It is handled by the UNCERTAIN class and by iteration-2 re-ranking under panel labels. A second possible confound: track-H-fitted fusion used on track L shares sentences? No: track H is FOLIO conclusions plus MALLS-test while track L candidates are FOLIO conclusions, so the SAME FOLIO sentences appear in both tracks, with different candidates. This is sentence leakage for the fused model and must be handled (see practice_alignment). HOW MUCH IS ENOUGH. Factuality meta-evaluation (TRUE, Honovich et al. NAACL 2022; SummaC, Laban et al. TACL 2022; FRANK, Pagnoni et al. NAACL 2021) reports ROC AUC on hundreds to thousands of labelled items per benchmark. With about 300 positives and 300 negatives the AUROC SE is about 0.02, and a paired ΔAUROC of about 0.05 is detectable. With 50/50 the MDE is about 0.10, and below that a stratum is descriptive only. The screen will have on the order of 100-300 track-L errors (3 systems × ~200 conclusions, error rate TBD), so it can rank candidates but not certify small deltas. Seeds: deterministic scoring; bootstrap B=2000 clustered by sentence (Deutsch, Dror & Roth TACL 2021 on resampling in metric meta-evaluation). MEASURES AND REPORTING. Item-level AUROC plus AUPRC with the prevalence stated, and precision at a pre-registered threshold. Precision must be quoted with its prevalence, or re-weighted to fixed prevalences. Tie-aware pairwise accuracy with tie calibration for quantised metrics (Deutsch, Foster & Freitag EMNLP 2023, 'Ties Matter'). FRANK-style per-error-type sensitivity. Invariance (false alarms under meaning-preserving rewrites). Coverage with failures in the denominator. Cost per item. System-level Kendall τ is reported only descriptively, since 3 systems give no power for it (TRUE's point). For QA-style factuality metrics (QAGS, QuestEval), the norm is also to report the answerer's own accuracy, which here is the L3 per-field gate. The decomposed-judge ablation mirrors it on the formula side.\","]}], "userModified": false, "replaceAll": false}

```
Plan for the FOL-Triage screen (Exp A), written 2026-09-23. It follows [[project_nl2fol_faithfulness_genstrat_iter1_run_u75jRHUss0zo]] and builds on [[project_nl2fol_faithfulness_genhypo_iter3_run_u75jRHUss0zo]].

**Code facts, verified in iter_3/gen_hypo/claude_agent/probes**
- The fol.py bug is `PREC xor=1`. The fix is `{'iff':1,'imp':2,'xor':3,'or':3,'and':4}`.
- repair_census.py has no `equivalent_modulo_vocab` or `minimal_typed_repair` functions. They have to be wrapped from `align(cand, ref)`, `gran_bridge` and `search(base, tgt, budget_s=25)`, following the steps in `work()`.
- The Logic-LM FOLIO_dev files exist for 3 systems. A planning fetch reported 63 records, which may be truncation, so the executor must log the actual count.

**Plan choices**
- Labels:
  - EQUIV and VOCAB → CORRECT.
  - GRAN and COMPOUND → UNCERTAIN. `auto_class` is emitted as well, so the label can be remapped later.
- Strata are computed from the REFERENCE formula.
- The same FOLIO conclusions appear in both track H and track L. The primary fusion model is therefore refit on H with every track-L sentence excluded.
- L3 uses the gemini-2.5-flash-lite class, text-only. Estimated spend is about $2.5; the hard stop is $8.
- The decomposed judge is an LLM reading the formula, compared against z3 answers.
```

### [72] ASSISTANT · 2026-09-23 13:00:25 UTC

```
I've written the plan for the FOL-Triage screening experiment to `.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`, with a `README.md` and an empty `.aii/manifest.yaml` beside it (this folder holds no heavy files). I couldn't run a JSON parser here: I only checked the file's layout by pattern search (9 top-level fields, no stray line breaks).

**Reused from earlier rounds:**
- The iteration-3 data and probe code.
- The parser `fol.py`, with a planned one-line fix: `PREC` changes to xor=3, so ⊕ binds like ∨ and tighter than →. The iteration-3 review traced three false COMPOUND labels to this bug.
- `repair_census.py`. It has no `equivalent_modulo_vocab` or `minimal_typed_repair` functions, so the plan has the executor wrap them from `align`, `gran_bridge` and `search`.
- `lint_smells.py` (the frozen L1 checks) and `content_accounting.py` (the bag-of-words L2 baseline).
- I confirmed the three Logic-LM FOLIO-dev output files exist and checked their format.

**What the executor will do:**
- **Screen:** rebuild the shared screen deterministically, following the strategy's definition to the letter: same item ids, same labels, same strata, same 150-item invariance set.
- **Labels:** GRAN and COMPOUND items become UNCERTAIN, and the raw labeller class is saved next to each label so iteration 2 can remap if another experiment coded them differently.
- **The four functions:**
  - `fol_lint`: the frozen iteration-3 smell list.
  - `content_accounting` plus `role_accounting`: dependency-parse roles checked against which predicates z3 places in condition vs asserted positions, a negation rule, and subject/object slot order.
  - `formula_role_profile`: exact z3 answers to a fixed role schema.
  - `role_questionnaire`: a cheap model (Gemini Flash-Lite class) that sees only the sentence and fills the same schema.
- **No peeking at labels:** everything is pre-registered in `prereg.json`, with code hashes, before any track-L label is joined, and the scoring code cannot read labels. Thresholds are set on known-correct formulas only.
- **Questionnaire gate:** fields below 70% accuracy are dropped from fusion, and the drop is logged.
- **Ablation:** the same model reads the formula instead of z3, which shows what exact solver answers add.
- **Contamination check:** the questionnaire is rerun on sentences with content words replaced by nonsense words.
- **Budget:** about $2.50 of OpenRouter spend expected, hard stop at $8, CPU only (`cpu_plus`).

**One gap I closed that the strategy didn't mention:** the same FOLIO conclusions appear in both tracks. A fusion model fitted on the human-error track (H) and applied to the LLM-output track (L) could therefore learn sentence-specific signal. The primary model is refit on track H with every track-L sentence excluded; the all-H model is reported as secondary.

**Risks:**
- A planning fetch reported only 63 records in a Logic-LM file, which may just be truncation. The executor logs the real count, and the strategy's premise extension covers a short file.
- This experiment alone cannot claim FOL-Triage beats an LLM judge. That comparison needs experiment D's scores, joined by item id in iteration 2.
- The long, heavily conditioned sentences the user cares most about cannot be tested on this screen; they are left to the held-out dataset.
- The labels are automatic, with no panel adjudication yet.

I also saved a memory note with the parser fix, the labeller wrappers and the leakage guard.
```
